I don't see things that aren't there.\n\nDoctor: Do you think the voice might be mistaken about your husband?\n\nPatient: I... no, I don't think it's mistaken. I know it sounds really far-fetched, but I’m still convinced my husband and the shop owner where I work have been tracking me. They use the EV chargers and the security cameras to watch exactly what I’m doing. They try to tell me I'm wrong, and they even show me the footage and say there’s nothing there, but... I know what they're doing. So, no, I don't think the voice is wrong about him.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your husband and the shop owner are really tracking you, even when others tell you there's no evidence of this?\n\nPatient: Yes, I do. They can pull up the security footage and point at the screen and tell me there's nothing there all they want, but... I know they're using those cameras and the EV chargers at the shop to watch my every move. I mean, I feel like I explain things pretty clearly. I know people don't always believe what I'm telling them about what's going on at the shop, but that's not because I can't get my thoughts straight. I don't have any trouble finding the words or making sense when I talk.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are connected and make sense when you're talking about the voice and the tracking, or do they feel a bit disjointed?\n\nPatient: No, they don't feel disjointed at all. I feel like everything is completely connected. When I'm talking about the cameras or what my husband is doing, it all makes perfect sense to me. I know exactly how the pieces fit together, and I can explain it clearly. It's just... well, like I said, it's just that other people refuse to believe it, not that I'm not making sense when I talk about it.\n\nDoctor: Do you think your belief about your husband and the shop owner tracking you could be wrong?\n\nPatient: No, I don't think I could be wrong. I mean, I know it sounds completely far-fetched, but... Even when they physically show me the footage and tell me there's nothing there, it doesn't change anything for me. I just know it's happening. So, no, I don't believe I'm wrong about this.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like your thoughts are racing or speeding up, making it hard to keep track of what you're thinking?\n\nPatient: No, not really. I mean... I don't feel like my thoughts are racing or going too fast. I can keep track of what I'm thinking about just fine. They just kind of move at a normal speed, I guess, so I haven't had any issues with that.\n\nDoctor: Do you have any other beliefs that you're absolutely certain of, even if others disagree or can't find evidence to support them?\n\nPatient: No, not really. He tells me not to trust my husband, or he'll specifically tell me not to touch certain cars in the shop. I know what they're doing. It usually comes from the vents at the garage where I work, or sometimes right out from under the hood of a vehicle. It's as clear as somebody standing right next to me. He tells me not to trust my husband, or he'll specifically tell me not to touch certain cars in the shop. He doesn't really tell me to go out and do other things, it's more just... telling me who to avoid or what vehicles to stay away from.\n\nDoctor: Does the voice ever tell you to do something that you feel you must do?\n\nPatient: No, I wouldn't say it's like that. It's more of a warning, I guess. He doesn't command me to do anything, he just tells me to stay away from certain things. I don't feel forced, it's just... I usually listen because it makes me nervous not to.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe what the voice tells you is true?\n\nPatient: I mean, I don't know if it's true, exactly. I just... I don't know why he would lie to me. It feels real enough that I don't want to risk ignoring it. Like, what if he's right about the cars, or my husband? I just... I know it sounds really far-fetched, but I’m still convinced my husband and the shop owner where I work have been tracking me. They use the EV chargers and the security cameras to watch exactly what I’m doing. They try to tell me I'm wrong, and they even show me the footage and say there’s nothing there, but... I know what they're doing. I know what they're doing.
